Town weather and approximate city data
Weather forecast: MET Norway Locationforecast https://api.met.no/
City lookup: Open-Meteo Geocoding (GeoNames) https://open-meteo.com/
Data license: CC BY 4.0 https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by/4.0/
Terms: https://api.met.no/doc/TermsOfService and https://open-meteo.com/en/terms
Weather is transformed into visual rain/snow categories and calendar seasons; it does not indicate provider endorsement.
